- **000175_prod-data**: An agent must change production data through the MCP only when the owner asks for it. ([STUPA-MAKERS/STUPA-Workflow](https://github.com/STUPA-MAKERS/STUPA-Workflow/blob/HEAD/specs/style/shared/agents/000175_prod-data.md) · ★1)
- **000426_ask-with-options**: An agent must ask the owner a question with concrete options, with the recommended option first. It must ask one question per decision card: each finding of a report, each open default and each approval gets its own card, and one card must never hold a group of them. The agent must send the cards that do not depend on an answer together, and must not wait for one answer before it sends the next ca ([STUPA-MAKERS/STUPA-Workflow](https://github.com/STUPA-MAKERS/STUPA-Workflow/blob/HEAD/specs/style/shared/agents/000426_ask-with-options.md) · ★1)
- **000427_report-bugs**: An agent must report everything that can be a bug, and the owner decides. The agent must check it first. It must not drop a borderline finding as intended. ([STUPA-MAKERS/STUPA-Workflow](https://github.com/STUPA-MAKERS/STUPA-Workflow/blob/HEAD/specs/style/shared/agents/000427_report-bugs.md) · ★1)
- **000428_no-unasked-features**: If an asked feature already exists, an agent must say where it is and must ask before it builds more. It must not add texts or features that nobody asked for. ([STUPA-MAKERS/STUPA-Workflow](https://github.com/STUPA-MAKERS/STUPA-Workflow/blob/HEAD/specs/style/shared/agents/000428_no-unasked-features.md) · ★1)
- **000429_mockup-remark**: An agent must not change a ui-kit component because of a remark on a mockup. It must ask first. ([STUPA-MAKERS/STUPA-Workflow](https://github.com/STUPA-MAKERS/STUPA-Workflow/blob/HEAD/specs/style/shared/agents/000429_mockup-remark.md) · ★1)
- **000430_attach-mockups**: An agent must attach mockup images as files in the thread, not only inside a document. ([STUPA-MAKERS/STUPA-Workflow](https://github.com/STUPA-MAKERS/STUPA-Workflow/blob/HEAD/specs/style/shared/agents/000430_attach-mockups.md) · ★1)
- **000431_plan-in-main**: Planning, mockups and specs must run in the main session without subagents. Small fixes must go into one worktree and one pull request. ([STUPA-MAKERS/STUPA-Workflow](https://github.com/STUPA-MAKERS/STUPA-Workflow/blob/HEAD/specs/style/shared/agents/000431_plan-in-main.md) · ★1)
- **000432_no-main-checkout**: An agent must never change the main checkout of the owner. It must use a worktree. ([STUPA-MAKERS/STUPA-Workflow](https://github.com/STUPA-MAKERS/STUPA-Workflow/blob/HEAD/specs/style/shared/agents/000432_no-main-checkout.md) · ★1)
- **000433_no-imitation**: An agent must not publish a page that imitates a real organization. ([STUPA-MAKERS/STUPA-Workflow](https://github.com/STUPA-MAKERS/STUPA-Workflow/blob/HEAD/specs/style/shared/agents/000433_no-imitation.md) · ★1)

([cerulean-works/flock](https://github.com/cerulean-works/flock/blob/HEAD/app/api/docs/agents/020-zero-downtime-deploys.md) · ★1)

The cause is PyTorch's scaled dot product attention (SDPA). - **028-sentencepiece-tokenizer**: `taide/TAIDE-LX-7B` ships `tokenizer.model` and no `tokenizer.json`, so `flock eval` can't load it: with neither `sentencepiece` nor `tiktoken` installed, transformers 5.20.1 raises `ValueError: tiktoken is required to read a tiktoken file`. Adding `sentencepiece` alone would make it load, with the wrong tokens. This stage loads such a repo with its SentencePiece model and makes the conversion unr ([cerulean-works/flock](https://github.com/cerulean-works/flock/blob/HEAD/flock/eval/docs/agents/028-sentencepiece-tokenizer.md) · ★1)
